<h2 class="title">
<a href="/en/product-highlight/s/stackpole-electronics/mlfa-series-metal-film-melf-resistor">AEC-Q200 compliant metal film MELF resistors</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/en/supplier-centers/s/stackpole-electronics" target="_self">Stackpole</a> offers the MLFA AEC compliant metal film MELF resistors available in 0.4W 0204 and 1W 0207 sizes. They offer exceptional pulse power handling and stability ideal for applications including metering, industrial controls, engine controls and telecom infrastructure.</p>
<a class="btn primary" href="/en/product-highlight/s/stackpole-electronics/mlfa-series-metal-film-melf-resistor">Learn More</a>
